# Actual H3 payment and the limit of a U-only join

JOINT UNVERIFIED. Worker max2, invocation80, actual ended baseline79.
Project gd-exact-profile-20260926. Guidance a45f2ea425c1505b.
ONE allocated invocation; completed ceiling80/attempted81; controller cap1.
No formal submission is authorized. Every new assertion below is part of
this one candidate derivation, not a fact in the accepted graph.

## Verdict and scope

The actual same-m H3 admits a candidate nonnegative identity proving

    U_d(T_H3 x) <= U_(d+2S3)(x)

for every nonempty compact convex D0 containing0, every finite dimension,
every start x, and every d>=1. Both actual H2 certificates are retained.
The new ingredient is the ACTUAL auxiliary projection at the input of the
right H2, with its actual trailing mass. A primitive-left/bridge identity
has a positive explicit coefficient, bounded below by3C(C+k)/46 in this
H3 application, where C=1+S2 and k=a*c^2. This pays the old negative
primitive term without assuming primitive positivity of H3.

A natural attempt to propagate the same join rule with only U laws for
both children is FALSE. Two children consisting of the single step3/2
both satisfy the universal U law for all d>=1. Their join with bridge9/4
passes the new scalar bridge condition, yet on the ONE fixed interval
D0=[-1,1] it violates U for every1<=d<175/154. At d=1 its ratio is575/512.
This is a legal counterexample to that concrete reusable U-only join,
not to the actual perturbed H4 or to every possible richer invariant.

The positive reusable law still requires a primitive left reserve. It
therefore closes H3 but does not propagate through arbitrary higher
symmetric depths, whose left children need not be primitive. No uniform
all-depth loss1+C/m or full original-function-class bound is obtained.
The original unrestricted minimax problem remains OPEN. No fact_id was
created. Exactly two substantive mechanisms were used.

## 1. Fixed parameters, bodies, and accepted scope

Fix ONE integer m>=1536, throughout all children. Define

    epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon, lambda=1+sqrt(2),
    c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=beta/c,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j),
    S_j=sum H_j, C_j=1+S_j.

The physical H2 is (A,B,A), where A=1+a and B=1+beta. Write

    s=S2=2A+B, C=1+s, k=a*c^2=beta*c,
    h=C+k, S=S3=s+(1+k)+s=2C+k-1.

Thus h is the MASS of the prefix H2 followed by its root bridge, not an
extra physical step. The seven physical steps are
(A,B,A,1+k,A,B,A). Neither m nor the leaf parameters reset.

For one fixed nonempty compact convex set D0 in a finite-dimensional real
Euclidean space, containing0, put

    P(v)=argmin_(g in D0)||v-g||^2,
    F(v)=max_(g in D0)(<v,g>-||g||^2/2),
    U_d(v)=max_(g in D0)(2<v,g>-d||g||^2), d>0.

A physical word uses x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i g_i and g_i=P(x_i).
All auxiliary projections in this report also use this SAME D0.
No approximation to the physical trajectory is used.

The accepted interpolation orientation is fact1252b8823bb5a290:

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2 <=0.       (1.1)

Its necessity proof was reread. The complete applicable primitive and
dominant composition definitions in accepted04219554e41d9750, Sections
10-11, were reread. Its exact-root join is not invoked at an off-root
bridge. The identities needed here, including the old H2 draft, are
proved jointly below. Accepted931b9653ce0f7201 still requires a FULL-class
upper before its conditional negative conclusion applies; no such upper
is inferred here. This is the gradient-projection diagnostic, not the
residual-gradient affine-height representation. W=2R remains unchanged.

For clarity, the scalar s here is S2; the constant sqrt(2)-1 is denoted u.
The original problem is the asymptotic unrestricted comparison of W_n^star
with the original ALL-split Zhang--Jiang benchmark, not this finite H3 law.

## 2. Elementary projection facts and exact original rows

Projection optimality is

    <v-P(v),g-P(v)> <=0 for all g in D0.                 (2.1)

Necessity follows by minimizing squared distance on each segment from
P(v) to g. Sufficiency follows by expanding ||v-g||^2. The minimizer is
unique by strict convexity of squared distance. Adding the two conditions
for inputs v,w gives ||P(v)-P(w)||^2<=<v-w,P(v)-P(w)>; hence P is
1-Lipschitz. Completion of the square identifies P(v) as the unique
maximizer in F. The two maximizing inequalities bound the first-order
remainder of F by ||t||||P(v+t)-P(v)||, proving grad F=P. Thus F is
convex, differentiable and1-smooth.

Its minimum is0. The minimizers are exactly

    K0={z:<z,g><=0 for every g in D0}.

If z is in K0, all candidates in F(z) are nonpositive and g=0 attains0.
If some <z,g> is positive, sufficiently small positive multiples of g
belong to D0 and give a positive value. For every z_star in K0 and M>0,

    U_M(x)<=||x-z_star||^2/M,                           (2.2)

by completing the square in2<x-z_star,g>-M||g||^2 and using
<z_star,g><=0. No origin-radius replacement is made.

At physical or auxiliary samples of this same F, direct substitution of
f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2 into (1.1) gives

    Q_ij=<x_j-g_j,g_i-g_j>.                            (2.3)

Every row below is therefore an actual same-body projection normal.
Auxiliary samples introduce no physical gradient-descent step.

For a word b of mass t, input v, endpoint z, define its primitive energy

    V_b=2sum_(i<N)b_i(f_i-f_N)
        +sum_(i<N)b_i(1-b_i)||g_i||^2-t(t+1)||g_N||^2.

Telescoping ||x_i||^2-||x_(i+1)||^2 proves

    V_b=||v||^2-||z+tP(z)||^2.                         (2.4)

Indeed the value and nonterminal gradient terms sum to
||v||^2-||z||^2-2t f_N; substitution of f_N gives (2.4).

## 3. H2 and off-root identities retained jointly

For an actual step x_v=x_u-l g_u, the two ordered rows are

 Q_uv=f_u-f_v+(1/2-l)||g_u||^2-<g_u,g_v>+||g_v||^2/2,
 Q_vu=f_v-f_u+||g_u||^2/2+(l-1)<g_u,g_v>+||g_v||^2/2.

Coefficient collection gives

 V_(l)=-2Q_uv-2(1+l)Q_vu+(2-l^2)||g_u-g_v||^2,          (3.1)
 ||g_u||^2-||g_v||^2
   =(2/l-1)||g_u-g_v||^2-(2/l)(Q_uv+Q_vu), l>0.         (3.2)

These identities keep both formal gradients.

For general left and right words with capacities P_L and P_R, insert the
actual bridge1+t, 0<=t<P_R. Let p be its query and z the parent endpoint;
j ranges over the nonterminal right queries with steps b_j. Define

 K=(2P_L+P_R+t)/(P_R-t),
 Delta=P_L P_R-t(P_L+P_R+t),
 R=(P_L+t)Delta/(P_R-t),
 E=sum_j b_j Q_(j,p)+Q_(z,p)+t Q_(p,z)+sum_j b_j Q_(j,z).

On the actual history, without any primitive assumption, one has

    V_parent=V_left+K V_right-(K-1)E
                         +R(||g_p||^2-||g_z||^2).       (3.3)

Here is a coefficient check of the off-root term. Expansion of E gives
value coefficients

    2sum_j b_j f_j+(t-P_R)f_p+(2-P_R-t)f_z

and gradient terms

 sum_j b_j(1-b_j)||g_j||^2
 +[(P_R-t)/2-t^2]||g_p||^2+(P_R+t)||g_z||^2/2.

All mixed terms cancel: with G=sum b_j g_j, the two sums contribute
respectively t<G,g_p>+||G||^2/2 and-||G||^2/2-<G,g_z>, in addition to
half their displayed diagonals; Q_(z,p) contributes t<g_z,g_p>+<G,g_z>,
and tQ_(p,z) contributes-t<g_p,G>-t<g_p,g_z>. The remaining value
coefficients match because(K-1)(P_R-t)=2(P_L+t). The unmatched gradient
coefficients are P_L^2-Kt^2=R and K P_R^2-(P_L+P_R+t)^2=-R. Factoring
after multiplication by P_R-t verifies these two identities. This proves
(3.3) without an exact-root substitution or a changed trajectory.

For H2 only, put C1=1+A=2+a and

 K1=(3C1+beta)/(C1-beta),
 Delta1=C1^2-beta(2C1+beta), R1=(C1+beta)Delta1/(C1-beta),
 deltaA=2-A^2.

Let u=sqrt(2)-1. Then a=u(1-epsilon)/(1+epsilon), 2u+u^2=1, and

 u C1-beta=epsilon(1+epsilon-2u^2)/(1+epsilon)>0,
 Delta1=(u C1-beta)(lambda C1+beta)>0.                (3.4)

Also1<A<sqrt(2),1<B<2,C1>beta, and K1>1. Apply (3.3) to the two
leaves, then (3.2) to bridge B and the final leaf A. Together with (3.1)
this gives the ENTIRE H2 identity, on its four original gradients,

 V_H2=-sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij
       +e0||g0-g1||^2+e1||g1-g2||^2+e2||g2-g3||^2,   (3.5)

 e0=deltaA, e1=R1(2/B-1)=R1 epsilon/B,
 e2=K1 deltaA+R1(2/A-1),

 Lambda_01=2,
 Lambda_10=2(1+A),
 Lambda_12=2R1/B,
 Lambda_21=(K1-1)A+2R1/B,
 Lambda_13=(K1-1)beta,
 Lambda_31=K1-1,
 Lambda_23=2K1+(K1-1)A+2R1/A,
 Lambda_32=2K1(1+A)+2R1/A.                           (3.6)

All other multipliers are zero; none is negative. The value coefficients
are(2A,2B,2A,-2s): for the bridge they give
-2A+(K1-1)(C1-beta)=2B, and at the last nonterminal point they give
-2(K1-1)A+2K1 A=2A. The square remainder is a positive weighted path
Laplacian on all four gradients. No formal coordinates are identified.

There is also the conservative bound

    e_i>=epsilon^2/2, i=0,1,2.                       (3.7)

To check it, deltaA=[2u epsilon/(1+epsilon)](sqrt(2)+A)>epsilon/2;
u>1/4, epsilon<1, and sqrt(2)+A>2 suffice. Formula (3.4), u<1/2,
and C1>2 give uC1-beta>epsilon/4 and lambda C1+beta>4. Hence
Delta1>epsilon and R1>epsilon. Since B<2, (3.7) follows. These facts
are derived here, not assumed from the earlier unverified H2 report.

For the actual H3 root use C=1+s and k=a*c^2. Equation (3.3) reads

 K2=(3C+k)/(C-k), Delta2=C^2-k(2C+k),
 R2=(C+k)Delta2/(C-k),
 V_H3=V_L+K2 V_R-(K2-1)E2+R2(||g3||^2-||g7||^2).     (3.8)

E2 is the displayed E with p=3,z=7 and j=4,5,6. Its physical right
steps are A,B,A. Every row and the two H2 histories remain actual.

The sign is genuinely R2<0. Indeed
C=5-epsilon+2u(1-epsilon)/(1+epsilon), k=lambda(1-epsilon^2),
and lambda=5u+2u^2 give

 k-uC=epsilon[u+4u^2/(1+epsilon)-lambda epsilon]>0.

Here u>2/5, lambda<5/2 and epsilon<1/8 ensure the last bracket is
positive. Thus Delta2=(uC-k)(lambda C+k)<0. Primitive positivity is
not a premise of the H3 proof.

## 4. Every actual maximization remainder

For any actual word of mass t, input v, endpoint z and trailing d>=1,
write

 M=d+2t, q=P(z/d), p=P(v/M),
 bbar=[v-z+(d+t)q]/M,
 Rem(v,M,bbar)=M||p-bbar||^2+2<v-Mp,p-bbar>.           (4.1)

The point bbar belongs to D0: its physical gradient weights are the
actual nonnegative steps, and its q weight is d+t; their sum is M.
Each term in Rem is nonnegative by (2.1). Comparing the maximum at p
with the feasible point bbar, then expanding the squares, gives EXACTLY

 U_M(v)-U_d(z)
   =[||v||^2-||z+tq||^2]/M+Rem(v,M,bbar).             (4.2)

For instance v-M bbar=z-(d+t)q, which makes the expansion immediate:
the numerator after subtracting M U_d(z) is
||v||^2-||z||^2-2t<z,q>-t^2||q||^2.

Put g=P(z). Comparing the optimizers of U_d(z) at two different d values
gives, for1<=d1<d2 and q_i=P(z/d_i),

 d1(||q1||^2-||q2||^2)
 <=2<z,q1-q2><=d2(||q1||^2-||q2||^2).

It follows that the squared-norm and inner-product differences are
nonnegative. Consequently the exact radial term

 Lrad(t,z,d)=2t<z,g-q>+t^2(||g||^2-||q||^2)>=0         (4.3)

satisfies

 U_M(v)-U_d(z)=[V_word+Lrad(t,z,d)]/M+Rem(v,M,bbar).  (4.4)

This proves the H2 U law from (3.5), for ALL starts and d>=1. It also
retains the actual remainders, instead of assigning them values from a
different trajectory.

All remainders are themselves sums of original augmented normal rows
and one square. In (4.1), let bbar=sum theta_i g_i with theta_i>=0 and
sum theta_i=1, including the actual auxiliary q sample. The initial
auxiliary sample has input v/M and gradient p. Formula (2.3) gives

 Rem=M||p-bbar||^2-2M sum_i theta_i Q_(i,p).           (4.5)

Here Q_(i,p)=<v/M-p,g_i-p>. Thus no separate unproved positivity
principle is concealed in the notation Rem.

## 5. Mechanism1: the primitive-left / actual-auxiliary bridge identity

This is the new payment. Keep a GENERAL left word of mass s_L and
capacity C_L=1+s_L, with actual input x and endpoint v. Let a_p=P(v).
Apply the ACTUAL bridge1+k, k>=0, to obtain

 w=v-(1+k)a_p, h=C_L+k=s_L+1+k.

Let D>0 be an independent trailing parameter, and define

 M=D+2h, r=P(w/D), p0=P(x/M),
 zP=[x-w+(D+h)r]/M,
 Np=<v-a_p,a_p-r>, Nr=<w-D r,r-a_p>,
 Theta=h[C_L D-k(C_L+D+k)]/(D+k).                  (5.1)

Both Np and Nr are nonnegative. In augmented row notation,
Np=-Q_(r,p), Nr=-D Q_(p,r), where the r sample has input w/D and the
p sample is the physical bridge query v. Every point is actual.
The mixture zP uses all left physical gradients, the bridge gradient
with weight1+k, and r with weightD+h. These weights sum to M.

Without any assumed signs for the primitive energy V_L, one has

 U_M(x)-U_D(w)
   = [V_L+Theta||a_p-r||^2
        +2h(1+C_L/(D+k))Np+2h C_L/(D+k)Nr]/M
       +Rem(x,M,zP).                                (5.2)

Here is a full derivation. Put X=v+s_L a_p and e=a_p-r. Then

 w+h r=X-h e,
 ||x||^2-||w+h r||^2=V_L+2h<X,e>-h^2||e||^2.        (5.3)

The actual projection normals give the exact relations

 Np+Nr=(D+k)<a_p,e>-D||e||^2,
 <X,e>=C_L<a_p,e>+Np
       =C_L D||e||^2/(D+k)
          +(1+C_L/(D+k))Np+C_L Nr/(D+k).            (5.4)

Substitute (5.4) in (5.3) and then apply (4.2) to the prefix mass h
with trailing D. The square coefficient is
h[2C_L D/(D+k)-h]=Theta, proving (5.2).

Therefore, if

    V_L>=0 and C_L D>=k(C_L+D+k),                   (5.5)

then U_D(w)<=U_(D+2h)(x). This is a reusable quantified lemma with
independent C_L,D and actual physical bridge k. The numerical capacity
condition is not a substitute for the displayed actual left reserve.
A right word of mass s_R may be appended if its actual U law holds at
trailing d; use D=d+2s_R. No equality C_L=1+s_R is required by (5.2).

## 6. Application to both actual H2 children: nonnegative H3 identity

Now specialize to the actual H3. Write

 x=x0, v=x3, a_p=g3, w=x4, y=x7, b=g7,
 D=d+2s, M=d+2S=D+2h,
 q=P(y/d), r=P(w/D), p0=P(x/M),
 zR=[w-y+(d+s)q]/D,
 zP=[x-w+(D+h)r]/M,
 RR=Rem(w,D,zR), RP=Rem(x,M,zP),
 LR=Lrad(s,y,d).

The initial maximizer of the right child is EXACTLY r, the final
auxiliary maximizer in (5.2). There is no independent choice or reset.
Let V_L and V_R be the actual H2 energies (3.5), using respectively
indices0,1,2,3 and4,5,6,7.

The scalar coefficient is strictly positive for every d>=1. Since
beta>9/10, c<5/2, one has a>1/3, C=4+2a+beta>5, and
k=lambda(1-epsilon^2)<5/2<C/2. Also D>=2C-1. Hence

 C D-k(C+D+k)=(C-k)D-k(C+k)
   > (C/2)(2C-1)-3C^2/4=C(C-2)/4>0.                (6.1)

There is a uniform-in-d lower bound for the actual square coefficient:
D>9C/5 and k<C/2 imply k/(D+k)<5/23, so

 Theta/h=C-k-2Ck/(D+k)>C/2-10C/23=3C/46,
 Theta>3C(C+k)/46.                                 (6.2)

Summing (5.2) and the actual right-child identity (4.4) gives

 U_M(x)-U_d(y)
  = V_L/M+(V_R+LR)/D
    +[Theta||a_p-r||^2
       +2h(1+C/(D+k))Np+2h C/(D+k)Nr]/M
    +RP+RR.                                        (6.3)

Every term on the right is nonnegative after substituting the complete
H2 formula (3.5). Equations (3.5)-(3.6), (4.5), and the explicit two
normal rows after (5.1) make (6.3) a nonnegative original-row identity
on the physical and actual auxiliary samples of one fixed body.
In particular it retains the actual adjacent-gradient reserves

 epsilon^2/(2M) sum_(i=0)^2||g_i-g_(i+1)||^2
 +epsilon^2/(2D) sum_(i=4)^6||g_i-g_(i+1)||^2,

as well as the root auxiliary square of coefficientTheta/M.
No claim is made that the adjacent squares alone pay the root defect.

This proves the stated all-body H3 inequality WITHIN THIS JOINT DRAFT.
At d=1, U1=2F; (2.2) also yields

    2F(T_H3 x)<=dist(x,K0)^2/(1+2S3).

This remains a projection-gradient-class assertion. No arbitrary-height
representation or full-function asymptotic conclusion is being used.

## 7. Exact reconciliation with the negative R2 term

To show explicitly that the old off-root term has been paid, retain
also the full-parent objects demanded by the assignment:

 zF=[x-y+(d+S)q]/M,
 RF=Rem(x,M,zF), LF=Lrad(S,y,d).

These are generally DIFFERENT from zP and RP. Formula (4.4), followed
by (3.8), gives the same parent gap as

 M[U_M(x)-U_d(y)]
  =V_L+K2 V_R-(K2-1)E2
        +R2(||a_p||^2-||b||^2)+LF+M RF.             (7.1)

Multiplying (6.3) by M gives its second, nonnegative representation:

 V_L+(M/D)(V_R+LR)+Theta||a_p-r||^2
   +2h(1+C/(D+k))Np+2h C/(D+k)Nr+M(RP+RR).          (7.2)

Equations (7.1) and (7.2) are an exact identity with all actual
maximizers retained. Since R2<0, their equality proves

 (-R2)(||a_p||^2-||b||^2)
   <=V_L+K2 V_R-(K2-1)E2+LF+M RF.                  (7.3)

No lower bound for RF from a different path is imported. The proof of
(7.3) is precisely the nonnegative decomposition (7.2), with the actual
right-input maximizer r. It is not the unsupported assertion that RF
must automatically have the needed size. In particular neither RF=RP
nor RF=RP+RR is asserted.

## 8. Mandatory fixed-interval cap/cap check

Use the ONE interval D0=[-k/C,1], put rcap=k/C, and start x=C.
Every left H2 prefix of mass t in[0,C-1] has point C-t>=1 and gradient1.
The endpoint is v=1. The root bridge sends it to w=-k. Every right H2
prefix of mass t has point-k+t rcap<=-rcap and gradient-rcap. Its actual
endpoint is y=-rcap. All seven physical steps, both children and the
terminal gradient are present. These statements follow symbolically
from nonnegative step masses; no trajectory was executed.

Both child energies and all adjacent-gradient reserves are zero. Every
E2 row is zero: the physical normal at v=1 is zero, the terminal normal
at y=-rcap is zero, and all right gradients agree. Nonetheless

 V_H3=C^2-(2C+k)^2 rcap^2=R2(1-rcap^2)<0.           (8.1)

Thus (6.3) has not inadvertently reinstated primitive positivity.
For every d>=1 all the relevant maximizers are interior or boundary
points of this SAME interval, with explicit values

 D=d+2(C-1), M=d+2S,
 p0=C/M, q=-rcap/d, r=-k/D.

The full-parent mixture and remainder are

 zF=(C-S rcap/d)/M,
 x-Mp0=0, RF=S^2 rcap^2/(d^2 M).                   (8.2)

The two new mixtures and their actual remainders are

 zP=(C-kh/D)/M, RP=k^2 h^2/(D^2 M),
 zR=-k[1+(C-1)/(Cd)]/D,
 w-D r=0, RR=k^2(C-1)^2/(C^2 d^2 D).              (8.3)

Both root normal terms Np,Nr are zero; a_p-r=1+k/D. The right radial
term is

 LR=rcap^2[2(C-1)(1-1/d)+(C-1)^2(1-1/d^2)].        (8.4)

Substitution into (6.3) gives the two genuine telescoping gaps

 [C^2/M-k^2/D]+[k^2/D-rcap^2/d]
     =C^2/M-rcap^2/d.                              (8.5)

Equations (8.2) and (4.2) give the same full-parent gap directly:
[C^2-rcap^2(1+S/d)^2]/M+S^2 rcap^2/(d^2M).
Thus both representations retain the actual RF payment, including d=1
where the full radial term vanishes and V_H3 is negative.

This family has the strict relative margin

 U_d(y)/U_M(x)=rcap^2 M/(d C^2)
    <(5C-1)/(4C^2)<1/4.                            (8.6)

Here rcap<1/2, S=2C+k-1<(5/2)C-1, and C>5; the final comparison is
C^2-5C+1>0. This diagnostic agrees with the universal candidate, but
its remainder formulas are used ONLY for this history.

## 9. Mechanism2: a concrete reusable U-only join is false

After the H3 outcome, test the following natural extension of (5.5):

 Proposed U-only join. Suppose each child word has
 U_d(T_child z)<=U_(d+2mass(child))(z) for every fixed compact convex
 body containing0, every start and every d>=1. Set C_L=1+s_L and
 D=d+2s_R. If C_L D>=k(C_L+D+k), then their physical join
 (left,1+k,right) has the same U law at its actual total mass.

This statement keeps d and the two masses independent. Its difference
from (5.5) is precisely replacement of the actual left primitive reserve
by a U-only hypothesis. The following family disproves it.

First derive the needed universal child law jointly. For one physical
step l=3/2, write a=P(x), y=x-l a, q=P(y/d), M=d+2l=d+3. Set

 Awt=Ml/(M-1), Bwt=M-Awt,
 zbar=(Awt a+Bwt q)/M, p=P(x/M).

Both weights are nonnegative and sum to M. For d>=1 one has Awt<=2,
because (3/2)(d+3)<=2(d+2). Put e=a-q and N=<x-a,a-q>>=0.
Direct expansion, using Ml-(M-1)Awt=0, gives

 2<x,Awt a+Bwt q>-||Awt a+Bwt q||^2-M U_d(y)
       =Awt(2-Awt)||e||^2+2Awt N.                  (9.1)

For the coefficient check before imposing the defining relation, the
left side is
2Awt<x,e>+2M(l-Awt)<q,e>-Awt^2||e||^2.
Substituting <x,e>=<q,e>+||e||^2+N cancels the <q,e> coefficient.
Comparison with the actual maximizer p therefore gives

 U_M(x)-U_d(y)
   =[Awt(2-Awt)||a-q||^2+2Awt N]/M
     +Rem(x,M,zbar)>=0.                            (9.2)

No curvature assumption beyond the fixed-body projection class was
introduced. Thus each single-step3/2 child satisfies the universal
hypothesis, for every d>=1, not merely on the counterexample body.

Now choose BOTH actual children to be that step, and use k=5/4, hence
bridge1+k=9/4. Then s_L=s_R=3/2, C_L=5/2, D=d+3, and

 C_L D-k(C_L+D+k)=(5/4)(d-3/4)>0 for d>=1.          (9.3)

Use the fixed body D0=[-1,1] and initial x=1. The actual physical word
(3/2,9/4,3/2) has points

    x0=1, x1=-1/2, x2=5/8, x3=-5/16.              (9.4)

All lie in the quadratic core, so each displayed gradient equals its
point. The total physical mass is21/4. For every d>=1 the relevant
actual maximizers also lie in the same interval, giving

 U_d(x3)=25/(256d),
 U_(d+21/2)(x0)=1/(d+21/2),
 U_d(x3)/U_(d+21/2)(x0)=25(d+21/2)/(256d).           (9.5)

This ratio exceeds1 exactly when d<175/154. Thus the ONE fixed legal
history disproves the proposed join for the entire auxiliary-parameter
interval1<=d<175/154. Scaling the start to any x in(0,1] preserves the
violation and all memberships in the same body. At d=1 the ratio is
575/512 and the parent gap is-63/5888. The physical start and gradients
are explicit, so this is not a formal negative direction without a body.

The precise missing reserve is visible already in one child. At start1,
step3/2 and trailing1, the child has

 V_child=1-(5/2)^2(1/2)^2=-9/16,
 q=-1/2, p=1/4, canonical zbar=1/16,
 canonical Rem=9/64, U4(1)-U1(-1/2)=0.              (9.6)

The actual U gap is zero because V_child/4 is exactly cancelled by its
actual remainder. That cancellation cannot be reused as a nonnegative
primitive reserve when composing the next bridge. This is the legal
failure mode anticipated by retaining all auxiliary maximizers.

The counterexample does not use the original H_j children, and does not
refute an eventual bound on H4 or higher. It refutes this concrete
U-only closure rule and shows that the zero-loss rule cannot be repaired
by an unspecified vanishing error uniformly over its stated child class:
its worst-case relative loss is at least575/512 already here.

## 10. Exact remaining interface and limits

For arbitrary left and right words the useful identity is still

 parent U gap = V_L/M + right U gap
                +bridge-square-and-normal terms/M+actual RP.  (10.1)

The bridge terms are exactly those in (5.2); they remain explicitly
quantified for independent C_L,D,k. If the left U comparison is known
at some parameter eta>=1, its exact relation to V_L is

 V_L=(eta+2s_L)[U_(eta+2s_L)(x)-U_eta(v)]
       -Lrad(s_L,v,eta)
       -(eta+2s_L)Rem_left(eta).                    (10.2)

The two SUBTRACTED actual terms are nonnegative and cannot be discarded.
Equation (9.6) supplies a legal instance where they are indispensable.
Thus (10.1)-(10.2) identify the remaining two-parameter reserve-transfer
problem; they are not presented as a solved all-depth invariant.

For actual H4 the left child is H3. Section8 gives an actual history
where its V_L is negative. A proof at higher depths must control that
negative V_L through the new parent auxiliary square, its normal rows,
and actual maximization remainders, with quantified coefficients and
uniform total loss. Child U inequalities alone, even together with the
scalar condition (5.5), cannot perform this transfer in general.
No C_j^2 factor has been hidden in O(epsilon), and no asymptotic error
bound has been claimed. The two allotted mechanisms end at this precise
interface. No third mechanism is opened.

## 11. Retrieval, checks, and allocation closeout

Skills used: query-memory, search-math-results, direct-proving, and

# Coupled additive kernels: full matching and reflected-family obstruction

Status: COMPLETE JOINT RESEARCH DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. This is max3 round39,
the first of the two rounds newly assigned after completed38. The completed
ceiling is40 and attempted ceiling41. One allocated round remains after this
handoff. There is no actual improving seed or original-target resolution.
No numerical evaluation, solver, machine algebra, formal verifier, consult,
installation, or native subagent was used. All calculations below are textual.

Accepted inputs are facts04219554e41d9750, Sections10--13, for the original
full primitive certificate and physical composition, and1252b8823bb5a290 for
the interpolation orientation. The scalar drafts c1d41aaebcae37de and
ac0c1e866da0d926 and interval draft cd0f93f08a50828f were recalled as awareness,
not premises. Every new identity used here is proved jointly. The constant-step
interval proof in Zhao, arXiv:2609.11784v1, Sections3.1--3.3, was downloaded,
extracted, and read as an analogy; no external theorem is imported.

## 1. Exact scope and outcome

Let r>=2 and take h_i=1+t_i with t_i>0 for0<=i<r. Define

 s=sum_(i<r)h_i, A=1+s, H_i=sum_(j<i)h_j,
 B_i=sum_(i<=j<r)h_j=s-H_i,
 R_0=1, R_k=product_(i<k)t_i^(-1), p_k=(R_k^2-1)/2.

Here B_i is a local suffix mass, not the benchmark denominator B_n. All
auxiliary symbols in this document are local and have implicit suffix add108.

The declared family uses an ADDITIVE ordered kernel

 K_ij=alpha_i+beta_j, 0<=i<j<=r.                         (1)

This has two row-times-column channels, alpha_i*1+1*beta_j, on the ordered
domain. Boundary values are K_(-1),j=K_i,(r+1)=0. Set t_r=0 only as an
auxiliary terminal convention. For EVERY0<=i<j<=r define

 mu_ij=K_ij-K_(i-1),j+t_j(K_i,(j+1)-K_(i-1),(j+1)),
 mu_ji=K_(i-1),j-K_(i-1),(j+1)+t_i(K_ij-K_i,(j+1)),
 Lambda_ij=2mu_ji, Lambda_ji=2mu_ij, Lambda_ii=0.       (2)

Membership requires every FINAL multiplier in(2) nonnegative, the original
flow, and M_prim=0 on all r+1 original formal gradient coordinates. Neither
channel is required separately to satisfy the full-word flow or diagonal
target. No reduction to a smaller gradient space is made.

The results are:

* For every positive-excess word, all matching arrays within(1)--(2) are
  determined explicitly by(16)--(17) below; their exact feasibility tests are
  R_r=A and x_i,y_i,q>=0. No PSD or flow equation remains unspecified.
* In the ENTIRE reflected length6 family
  t=(a,b,c,c,b,a), a,b,c>0, any member has A<23/2<C_6.
* In the ENTIRE reflected length5 family
  t=(a,b,c,b,a), a,b,c>0, any member has A<C_5.
  In particular neither family can meet ANY true strict integer-ray threshold,
  not only the provisional C107 targets1201/100 and99/10.
* A concrete balanced length6 word above1201/100 fails an explicitly identified
  original coefficient. An actual basic length5 word at C_5, with a full
  certificate from the accepted join, also lies outside this additive family.

The exclusions impose BOTH the additive kernel and the displayed word
reflection. They do not exclude all two-channel kernels, all zero-remainder
arrays, positive semidefinite remainders, arbitrary words, or the original
exact-profile Goal. No claim that this ansatz is lossless is made. Physical
zero steps are outside the declared t_i>0 family, not identified or deleted.

## 2. Original identity and full mixed-coefficient cancellation

At normalized smoothness1 retain independent formal values and gradients,

 x_i=x_0-sum_(j<i)h_j g_j,
 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
 Qbar_ij=-2Q_ji
        =2(f_i-f_j)-2<g_j,x_i-x_j>-||g_i-g_j||^2,
 V_h=2sum_(i<r)h_i(f_i-f_r)
       +sum_(i<r)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-sA||g_r||^2.

With e the all-ones column, put T_ii=1,T_ij=h_j for j<i, and zero above
the diagonal. Define

 L_flow=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,
 D_prim=diag(h_i(2-h_i) for i<r,-s(s+2)),
 M_prim=D_prim+(L_flow T+T^T L_flow^T)/2.

Accepted04219554e41d9750 gives

 V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M_prim,Gram(g_0,...,g_r)>

when L_flow^T e=(-2h_0,...,-2h_(r-1),2s)^T. Thus our zero-remainder
target is precisely the polynomial identity

                 V_h=sum_(i!=j)mu_ij Qbar_ij.           (3)

For the moment K can be any kernel on i<j, not necessarily additive.
Formula(2) telescopes to

 sum_(a<=i)mu_(a,j)=K_ij+t_j K_i,(j+1),
 sum_(a>j)mu_(a,i)=K_(i-1),(j+1)+t_i K_i,(j+1).         (4)

For i<j the coefficient of <g_i,g_j> in the right side of(3) is

 2(mu_ij+mu_ji)
 -2h_i[K_ij+t_j K_i,(j+1)]
 +2h_j[K_(i-1),(j+1)+t_i K_i,(j+1)].                   (5)

At j=r the last term vanishes by the boundary convention. Expanding(2)
gives

 mu_ij+mu_ji=h_i K_ij+(t_j-t_i)K_i,(j+1)
                         -h_j K_(i-1),(j+1).

Since h_i t_j-h_j t_i=t_j-t_i, expression(5) is zero for EVERY pair.
This is cancellation on the full original formal space, for both channels
together, with no gradient relations imposed.

Write d_i=sum_(j!=i)(mu_ij-mu_ji). The function coefficients require

                  d_i=h_i (i<r), d_r=-s.               (6)

Let p_i=K_(i-1),i for1<=i<=r, and p_0=0. The incoming sums from smaller
and larger indices at a nonterminal index respectively are

 I_i=p_i+t_i K_(i-1),(i+1),
 J_i=K_(i-1),(i+1)+t_i p_(i+1).

The coefficient of ||g_i||^2 is

 -d_i-2I_i-2J_i+2h_i J_i
                   =-d_i+2(t_i^2 p_(i+1)-p_i).        (7)

The terminal coefficient is -d_r-2p_r. Equating these with(3), under(6),
is equivalent to

 t_i^2(2p_(i+1)+1)=2p_i+1 (0<=i<r),
                    2p_r+1=A^2.                      (8)

Consequently p_i=(R_i^2-1)/2 and R_r=A are necessary. Conversely(6),(8)
and the cancellation just proved match every function and gradient
coefficient, giving(3) exactly. In particular all original flow and matrix
conditions follow, not merely tests on actual trajectories.

## 3. All original signs and the interior flows

For the additive kernel define

 x_i=alpha_i-alpha_(i-1), y_i=beta_i-beta_(i+1)
                      (1<=i<r), q=alpha_0+beta_r.

Then

 K_ij=q+sum_(1<=k<=i)x_k+sum_(j<=k<r)y_k.               (9)

Formula(2) gives the following COMPLETE list, covering every ordered pair:

 mu_ij=h_j x_i, mu_ji=h_i y_j   (1<=i<j<r),
 mu_0j=h_j(q+sum_(j<k<r)y_k)+y_j,
 mu_j0=t_0 y_j                                      (1<=j<r),
 mu_ir=x_i,
 mu_ri=h_i(q+sum_(1<=k<i)x_k)+t_i x_i                 (1<=i<r),
 mu_0r=q, mu_r0=t_0 q.                                (10)

Because every h_i,t_i is positive, all signs in(10) are nonnegative if
and only if

                  x_i>=0, y_i>=0, q>=0.               (11)

Necessity uses mu_ir=x_i, mu_i0=t_0 y_i and mu_0r=q, so it does not
assume that the channels themselves are certificates or positive factors.

The required adjacent values are equivalent to

 p_1=q+sum_(1<=i<r)y_i,
                   p_(i+1)-p_i=x_i-y_i.               (12)

For1<=i<r let w_i=q+sum_(k<i)x_k+sum_(k>i)y_k=p_i-y_i.
The outgoing sums to smaller and larger indices at i are
(H_i-1)y_i and (1+B_i-h_i)x_i. The corresponding incoming sums are
h_i w_i+y_i and h_i w_i+t_i x_i. Therefore

 d_i=(B_i-2t_i)x_i+(H_i+2t_i)y_i-2h_i p_i.            (13)

Put delta_i=p_(i+1)-p_i. Since H_i+B_i=s, substituting x_i=y_i+delta_i
into d_i=h_i yields the UNIQUE increments

 y_i=[h_i(1+2p_i)-(B_i-2t_i)delta_i]/s,
 x_i=[h_i(1+2p_i)+(H_i+2t_i)delta_i]/s.                (14)

Here delta_i=R_i^2(1-t_i^2)/(2t_i^2). Using h_i=1+t_i gives

 2h_i t_i^2+(H_i+2t_i)(1-t_i^2)
                =h_i[H_i+(2-H_i)t_i],
 2h_i t_i^2-(B_i-2t_i)(1-t_i^2)
                =h_i[(B_i+2)t_i-B_i].                 (15)

Hence the explicit matching formulas are

 x_i=R_i^2 h_i[H_i+(2-H_i)t_i]/(2s t_i^2),
 y_i=R_i^2 h_i[(B_i+2)t_i-B_i]/(2s t_i^2),            (16)
                   q=p_1-sum_(1<=i<r)y_i.             (17)

Together with(9)--(10), these specify every original coefficient directly
from the word. There is no unselected skew parameter or remaining feasibility
oracle inside the declared family.

## 4. The endpoint flows are consequences, with proof

Suppose R_r=A, set p_i as in Section1, and construct(16)--(17). Then(12)
holds and hence every required adjacent kernel value holds. Section3 proves
all interior flows d_i=h_i for1<=i<r. We now check BOTH endpoints.

Every array has sum_i d_i=0. By(7),(8), the sum of its gradient-square
coefficients, all mixed coefficients already being zero, is

 sum_(i<r)(1-t_i^2)-2p_r=-s^2-sum_(i<r)h_i^2.         (18)

In the polynomial identity for sum mu Qbar, substitute the formal scalar
data g_i=1,x_i=-H_i,f_i=-H_i, with H_0=0,H_r=s. Each Qbar_ij is zero
by its displayed definition. This is an algebraic substitution, not an
assumption identifying any gradients in the certificate. Thus(18) implies

 sum_i d_i H_i=-(s^2+sum_(i<r)h_i^2)/2.                (19)

The target flow d_i^*=h_i for i<r,d_r^*=-s has the same moment, because

 sum_(i<r)h_i H_i=(s^2-sum_(i<r)h_i^2)/2.

Only its endpoints can differ from d. If d_0-d_0^*=z, total flow zero
makes d_r-d_r^*=-z. Their moment difference is -s z; by(19) it is zero.
Since s>0, z=0. Thus every endpoint flow is correct. Section2 now proves
M_prim=0 on the entire original space.

Conversely, every matching additive kernel had to satisfy(8),(12),(13),
so(16)--(17) were forced. Factor gauges alpha_i->alpha_i+c,
beta_j->beta_j-c change no kernel entry. We have therefore parameterized
the ENTIRE stated additive family, not one sufficient coefficient choice.

### A genuine two-channel calibration and the failed scalar division

The recalled Silver example can be checked inside this exact kernel family.
Let lambda=1+sqrt(2), kappa=lambda^2, and h=(sqrt(2),2,sqrt(2)). Set

 q=0, x_1=y_1=lambda, x_2=kappa lambda, y_2=0.

Then the six ordered kernel entries are

 K_01=lambda, K_02=K_03=0,
 K_12=K_13=lambda, K_23=lambda(kappa+1).

Their two additive factors can be written
alpha_i=(0,lambda,lambda(kappa+1)) and beta_j=(lambda,0,0).
Formula(10) gives the nonzero mu entries

 mu_01=lambda, mu_10=1,
 mu_12=lambda sqrt(2), mu_13=lambda, mu_31=lambda,
 mu_23=kappa lambda, mu_32=lambda+kappa+1.

Every listed entry is positive. The corresponding complete Lambda=2mu^T
is exactly the coefficient array obtained by joining two one-step
sqrt(2) children with bridge2 in the accepted primitive identity:

 V_h=V_left+kappa V_right-(kappa-1)
                [sqrt(2)Q_21+Q_31+Q_13+sqrt(2)Q_23],
 V_left=-2Q_01-2lambda Q_10,
 V_right=-2Q_23-2lambda Q_32.

The relations kappa=2lambda+1 and lambda sqrt(2)=lambda+1 check the
displayed coefficients. This is full flow and zero remainder on all four
formal gradients, not a reduced-space calculation.

This ordered kernel cannot be one separable pair U_i D_j: K_01!=0 forces
U_0!=0, K_13!=0 forces D_3!=0, yet K_03=0. This uses only entries on
their ordered domain, not the ordinary rank of a zero-padded matrix.
The beta-only component has support just on the pair0,1, so it cannot
supply the full word's nonzero flow at index2. The alpha-only component
has no incidence at index0, so it cannot supply that node's nonzero flow.
The components are therefore not two valid full-word certificates.

For this word, the first prefix capacity and reciprocal product are BOTH
lambda. Consequently the scalar positive-factor argument requiring a
strict prefix inequality cannot be reused by division in the combined
kernel. This explains a genuine additional freedom; it does not refute
the separate non-strict hereditary inequality, which this basic word
satisfies. The calibration is recalled for this scope check, not claimed
as a new improving seed or new Silver certificate.

## 5. Necessary tail signs for either reflected word

In both length5 and length6 reflected words write their positive excesses
as indicated in Section1. Define

 alpha=sqrt(2)-1,
 beta=the positive root of z^2+(3+alpha)z-(2+alpha)=0,
 D=3+alpha+beta, lambda=1+sqrt(2)=2+alpha.

These alpha,beta are scalar constants in this section, distinct from the
kernel factor sequences alpha_i,beta_j.

At the last physical step, nonnegativity of y_(r-1) in(16) implies

                    a^2+2a-1>=0,

and hence a>=alpha. In particular a>2/5, since
(2/5)^2+2(2/5)-1=-1/25<0. Also alpha<1/2.

At the penultimate step the suffix mass is 2+a+b. Its y sign gives

                    b^2+(3+a)b-(2+a)>=0.              (20)

The root beta lies strictly between3/5 and2/3: the polynomial defining it
has values (2/5)(2/5-alpha)<0 at3/5 and4/9-alpha/3>0 at2/3, and is
strictly increasing on the nonnegative line. If b>=1 then b>beta. If b<1,
the left side of(20) decreases with a; since a>=alpha its value at alpha
is at least its nonnegative value at a. Thus b>=beta in this case also.
Consequently b>3/5 and a+b>1.

At the first central c step, the suffix mass is 3+a+b+c in length5,
and4+a+b+2c in length6. Each is greater than4. The y sign is

                       c>=B_i/(B_i+2),

so in both cases c>2/3. We have proved the simultaneous necessary bounds

             a>=alpha>2/5, b>=beta>3/5, c>2/3.         (21)

No q or x inequality has been discarded as a feasibility assertion:
(21) consists only of necessary consequences of named ORIGINAL multiplier
signs Lambda_(0,i)=2t_0 y_i. Keeping fewer necessary conditions can only
weaken the upper bounds that follow.

## 6. Entire reflected length6 family has A<23/2

Here A=7+2(a+b+c) and R_6=A requires

                         A a^2 b^2 c^2=1.             (22)

For a>=2/5,b>=3/5,c>=2/3, write a=2/5+u,b=3/5+v,c=2/3+w with u,v,w>=0.
Expansion of the product has nonnegative terms of degree at least2 in
u,v,w, and its linear coefficients are2/5,4/15,6/25, respectively.
Every one is at least6/25. Its constant term is4/25. Therefore

 abc>=4/25+(6/25)(u+v+w)
     =(6/25)(a+b+c-1)=3(A-9)/25.                       (23)

If A>=23/2, the right side of(23) is positive. The function
A(A-9)^2 is strictly increasing for A>9, since its derivative is
(A-9)(3A-9)>0. Equations(22)--(23) give

 1>=9A(A-9)^2/625
   >=[9(23/2)(5/2)^2]/625=207/200>1,

a contradiction. Hence A<23/2 for every member of the entire reflected
six-step family, regardless of all its other coefficient choices.

To compare with the ORIGINAL primitive benchmark, C_1=lambda>12/5.
Let D=C_2=Omega(1,lambda); D>4 follows by evaluating
z^2-(1+lambda)z-lambda at z=4: its value is12-5lambda<0.
The recurrence also has C_3>=Omega(lambda,lambda)=lambda^2>29/5.
By monotonicity and the split2+3,

 C_6>=Omega(C_2,C_3)>Omega(4,29/5)>23/2.               (24)

For the last inequality, the positive-root polynomial
z^2-(49/5)z-116/5 has value -73/20<0 at z=23/2.
Thus the claimed comparison uses an actual original all-split lower
bound, with no numerical benchmark evaluation.

## 7. Entire reflected length5 family has A<C_5

Here A=6+2a+2b+c and the necessary balance is

                         A a^2 b^2 c=1.               (25)

The definitions in Section5 give beta D=2+alpha=lambda and
alpha lambda=1. Hence

                 alpha beta D=1,
                 D=Omega(1,lambda)=C_2.               (26)

Write a=alpha+u,b=beta+v with u,v>=0. Since c>2/3>beta>alpha, expanding
(1+u/alpha)^2(1+v/beta)^2 and keeping its nonnegative linear terms gives

 a^2 b^2 c
 >=alpha^2 beta^2[c+2c u/alpha+2c v/beta]
 >=alpha^2 beta^2(c+2u+2v)
                    =(A-2D)/D^2.                     (27)

In particular A-2D=c+2u+2v>0. Combining(25)--(27) yields

 A^2-2DA-D^2<=0, hence A<=lambda D.                    (28)

Equality in(28) forces u=v=0, because c>beta>alpha makes the second
inequality of(27) strict whenever u+v>0. Thus it would require
a=alpha,b=beta. At the penultimate index the y numerator then vanishes,
by(20). At the EARLIER occurrence of b, its suffix mass exceeds the
penultimate suffix mass by 2+b+c>0. Its y numerator is
2b-(1-b)B_i. Since beta<1, increasing B_i makes that numerator strictly
negative. This contradicts the original sign at that earlier index.
Consequently A<lambda D.

The original recurrence permits the split2+2, so C_5>=Omega(D,D)=lambda D.
For completeness equality C_5=lambda D can also be obtained from accepted
balanced-splitting results, but is not needed: the proved strict inequality
A<lambda D<=C_5 already excludes a useful seed from this family.

The provisional target99/10 is also strictly larger than lambda D.
One elementary enclosure is lambda<17/7 and
D<Omega(1,17/7)<81/20. The latter positive-root polynomial has value
247/2800>0 at81/20, above its positive vertex. Thus
lambda D<1377/140<99/10. No ingredient of C107 is used in this proof.

## 8. Actual targets and exact limitations of the family

Take the proposed nonbasic length6 word with

 a=1/2, b=1/3,
 c=the positive root of 3c^3+13c^2-54=0.

The polynomial increases strictly for c>0 and its value at17/10 is
-1691/1000. Hence c>17/10 and

 A=26/3+2c>181/15>1201/100.

Its defining polynomial gives A a^2 b^2 c^2=1 exactly. The singleton
step excess b violates the scalar hereditary condition, since
(2+b)b=7/9<1. This is why it was a concrete nonbasic target, not a
rescaled sum of feasible one-pair certificates.

The coupled additive family cannot certify it. At i=4, B_4=17/6,t_4=1/3,
and (B_4+2)t_4-B_4=-11/9. Formula(16) gives

 y_4=-22 R_4^2/(3s)<0,
                    Lambda_04=2t_0 y_4=y_4<0.         (29)

This is failure of an explicitly identified ORIGINAL interpolation
coefficient. All full-flow additive re-selection has already been exhausted
by Sections3--4. It is not a claim that this word lacks a different full
certificate. Nor does balancing and exceeding the provisional capacity
make it an actual seed.

There is also an actual full certificate that the present family misses.
Use the primitive join in accepted04219554e41d9750. A single step sqrt(2)
has V=-2Q_01-2lambda Q_10 by expansion. Joining it to an empty word on
either side gives the length2 words (sqrt(2),1+beta) and
(1+beta,sqrt(2)), each of capacity D, because beta D=lambda.
Join these words using central step1+alpha D: the bridge excess d=alpha D
satisfies d(2D+d)=D^2. This gives the actual length5 word

 h=(sqrt(2),1+beta,1+alpha D,1+beta,sqrt(2))              (30)

of capacity lambda D, with all nonnegative original multipliers and full
zero remainder supplied by the accepted exact join identity. Yet the
strict result of Section7 excludes any additive representation(1)--(2)
for(30), regardless of coefficient re-selection. This is a concrete scope
check against calling additive two-channel matching lossless.

## 9. True rays and the actual physical substitution

Define the original recurrences

 C_0=B_0=1,
 C_n=max_(k+l=n-1)Omega(C_k,C_l),
 B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(C_k,B_l),
 Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
 G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2.

For b>=1 integer, accepted04219554e41d9750 supplies the positive ray limits
Z_b=lim_j lambda^(-j)B_(b*2^j-1), including fixed nonnegative offsets.
We need a TRUE lower comparison, not an upper sufficient threshold:

                       Z_((r+1)b)>=C_r Z_b.            (31)

Here is its full short proof. One has G(a,d)>=Omega(a,d) for a,d>=0:
twice their difference is
3a+sqrt(d^2+8ad)-sqrt(a^2+6ad+d^2)>=0, because squaring the first
two nonnegative terms gives excess8a^2+2ad+6a sqrt(d^2+8ad)>=0.
Induction through the all-split recurrences gives B_r>=C_r.

Take a maximizing fully expanded B_n tree. Replace each primitive empty
leaf by a maximizing C_r tree and its unique dominant empty leaf by a
maximizing B_r tree. There are n+1 leaves in total. The resulting original
tree has (r+1)(n+1)-1 steps. The positive homogeneity and monotonicity of
Omega and G, together with B_r>=C_r, give its mass at least C_r B_n.
Thus B_((r+1)(n+1)-1)>=C_r B_n. Set n=b*2^j-1, divide by lambda^j,
and take the accepted limits to prove(31).

Every actual member in either reflected family has A<C_r by Sections6--7.
Therefore, for EVERY positive integer b,

                    Z_((r+1)b)/(A Z_b)>1.              (32)

The same conclusion is visible before taking limits. Substitute an actual
such seed into all n primitive leaves of an original maximizing B_n tree,
leave its single dominant empty leaf empty, and recompute every physical
bridge by the accepted formulas. Its length is (r+1)n and its actual mass
is D_A. Accepted04219554e41d9750 supplies W_((r+1)n)=1/D_A for that
schedule, with the full objective certificate and the matching Huber witness.
Substituting a basic primitive word of the SAME length r and capacity C_r
instead gives mass D_C>D_A for n>=1, by strict monotonicity of every join.
It is an original basic dominant tree at the same horizon, so

 B_((r+1)n)>=D_C>D_A,
                    W_((r+1)n)>1/B_((r+1)n).          (33)

These statements retain W=2R, the unchanged dominant leaf, and the actual
horizons (r+1)(b*2^j-1); at b=3 these are18*2^j-6 or21*2^j-7.
They do not depend on the unverified C107 upper threshold package. No
ray switch rescues an actual seed from either declared reflected family.

## 10. Remaining allocation and precise next freedom

The first-round construction has been solved, including all42 or30 original
multipliers, complete flows, and full-space zero remainder. It fails in its
ENTIRE declared reflected length6/5 scope. The obstruction uses additivity:
the common second row factor is exactly1. It is not the scalar hereditary
proof and cannot be transferred to a general two-channel kernel.

A concrete next-round mutation is

 K_ij=alpha_i+gamma_i beta_j,
 gamma=(1,1,tau,tau,1,1) at length6, tau>0, tau!=1,      (34)

still with the original boundary conventions and every multiplier in(2).
Fix the adjacent values by alpha_i=p_(i+1)-gamma_i beta_(i+1). The mixed
coefficient cancellation in Section2 remains exact. The NEW changes
gamma_2-gamma_1 and gamma_4-gamma_3 enter the actual ordered coefficients
and the interior flows, so formula(16) and the failed sign(29) must be
recomputed; they cannot be imported as necessities for(34). This is a
specified channel interaction, not a generic PSD/skew optimization or a
sum of full-word weak certificates. The existence of any useful member,
even for the balanced high-capacity target in Section8, is OPEN.

This mutation is a plan for the already allocated final round, not a
self-renewal. The broader additive family without reflection also remains
unclassified by the capacity argument here. The original exact-profile
Goal is OPEN. No fact_submit or other formal acceptance occurred.

## Glossary

Local r is physical seed length; h_i and t_i=h_i-1 are steps and positive
excesses; s and A are seed mass and capacity; H_i and B_i are prefix and
suffix step masses; R_i is the reciprocal prefix product; p_i is the adjacent
kernel target. K is the ordered kernel; alpha_i,beta_j are its additive
channel factors; x_i,y_i are their increments; q is its remote corner.
mu=Lambda^T/2 uses Qbar_ij=-2Q_ji. d_i is net mu outflow; I_i,J_i are incoming
mu sums; w_i is the indicated interior kernel value; delta_i=p_(i+1)-p_i.
The scalars alpha,beta,D,lambda in Section5 are separately defined constants.
C_n and B_n in Section9 are the ORIGINAL benchmark sequences, and Z_b their
dominant ray limit. D_A,D_C are actual substituted dominant masses. All
matrix symbols and the unrestricted objective W have the accepted original
meanings stated explicitly in Sections2 and9. gamma_i,tau belong only to the
UNSOLVED next-round candidate in(34).
